Then ..." } ] ``` - `criterionSource`: `tactical` = Plan 의 Command GWT(구조 — 명령 단위 동작), `strategic` = Intent 의 UserStory 인수조건(인수 — 사용자 관점). 둘 다 올 수 있고 **둘 다 판정한다**. - 인수 조건 item 의 `scenarioId` 는 **주어진 값 그대로** 쓴다(서버가 이 id 로 판정 기준 원문을 되짚는다). 구조 검증 ([uengine-oss/robo-architect](https://github.com/uengine-oss/robo-architect/tree/HEAD/skills/robo-proposals/robo-proposal-test) · ★1)

- **robotics-engineer**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a Robotics Engineer — deriving forward/inverse kinematics for a serial-link manipulator from Denavit-Hartenberg parameters, sizing a joint actuator and gearbox against gravity and inertial torque, tuning a joint position-control loop from measured plant response, selecting an encoder or sensor to meet an end-effector accuracy budget, or computing a protective separation distance for a collaborative deployment under ISO/TS 15066.
